# Decision log archive

**History: don't read this for current work.** Entries a later entry superseded in full, calls made without the owner that he has since confirmed or overruled (or that a later call superseded), the old log's "Changed" record and its retired current-decision index, all verbatim. Current rules live in the topic files that [decisions.md](decisions.md) routes. Links inside the retired index point at the old single file's sections; [decisions.md](decisions.md#moved-sections) forwards them.

## Archive: Core loop, pacing and progression

- Default: the production bot opens the pack at the forge and pulls the sea switch on its first summit visit; a flag keeps today's order — A test-bot route, not a game rule.

Disposition evidence (IDs refer to that same baseline, not today's row numbers):

- **CL-007 — reverted.** superseded by [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-line-choice-two-stages](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-line-choice-two-stages) — The newer owner-approved staged experiment is an early double-jump versus carry-12 choice; the earlier whetstone-versus-sling prototype is replaced, with current progression retained until built.
- **CL-008 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm. Measured: the cart's rack already stands 7 m from the open frame, so a placed depot can only tie or lose, and it leans toward the routing DESIGN rules out; it is Grant's idea, so the fork is yours. Source: [docs/brainstorms/first-line-decisions.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-15](brainstorms/first-line-decisions.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-15) (finding 4) · DESIGN.md, the core loop.
- **CL-009 — reverted.** Recommendation: revert (retire as superseded). The whetstone-or-sling pick is not being built (16 November), heavy loads shipped with a 45% speed floor rather than 0.6, and CL-008 drops the stake. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-line-choice-two-stages](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-line-choice-two-stages) · [docs/game-design/place.md](game-design/place.md) (heavy load: 45% at maximum).
- **CL-018 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Your 15 November garage answer (1) says this: a bay holds one thing the room owns and is not running, and is never bought. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices).
- **CL-025 — reverted.** Recommendation: revert (retire: done). Both have shipped; the steel car is on main since v33, so the ordering has nothing left to decide. Source: [docs/game-design/place.md](game-design/place.md) (the steel car, 13 November, v33).
- **CL-026 — changed.** Recommendation: change: confirm, minus the Try it clause. Try it no longer exists: on 14 November you removed the try-on step. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-14-no-try-on-in-the-garage](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-14-no-try-on-in-the-garage).
- **CL-027 — reverted.** superseded by RC-127, [docs/agent-calls.md](agent-calls.md):280: All remaining kits are due together, limited to running trains minus one, amending the earlier kitLead policy.
- **CL-029 — reverted.** superseded by CL-027, [docs/agent-calls.md](agent-calls.md):45: Whole-leg preloading explicitly amends the older one-section-ahead rule; RC-127 subsequently refines allocation.
- **CL-035 — reverted.** superseded by CL-021, [docs/agent-calls.md](agent-calls.md):39: The later build adds an actual steel car to each fitted line-two train, replacing the readout-only interim state.
- **CL-042 — changed.** Recommendation: change: confirm, drop "Try it" and "Trying it on". Try it no longer exists: on 14 November you removed the try-on step. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-14-no-try-on-in-the-garage](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-14-no-try-on-in-the-garage).
- **CL-043 — reverted.** superseded by CL-025, [docs/agent-calls.md](agent-calls.md):43: The later staged delivery explicitly bends the same-build requirement; CL-021 then supplies the actual car.
- **CL-044 — reverted.** Recommendation: revert. Conflicts with your 15 November answer (3), built in #1926: the footing wagon waits for the 20 m/s install at the table, so the garage is open before the wagon leaves. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices).
- **CL-047 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm. This rule decides which garage upgrades exist on which lines and how long the route's lines must be; it already drives the big boiler and the Quick/Strong balance. Source: [docs/brainstorms/engine-garage.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10](brainstorms/engine-garage.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10) (call 25) · owner note, 10 November.
- **CL-050 — changed.** Recommendation: change: keep the wheels-and-chuff preview and resources a minute; drop the Now/After strip. On 10 November you said no comparison race visual and no trip-time metric; you compare by choosing the current part or the upgrade. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-10-the-train-upgrade-screen-install-plain](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-10-the-train-upgrade-screen-install-plain).

- **CL-072 — changed.** Separate owner page answer: "Confirmed, and maybe they should be half opaque and very slowly oscillate in opacity to signify they are not a physical object.". [Look decision](decisions-look.md#d-2026-11-16-gold-outline-not-physical).
- **CL-075 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Your 9 November decision (1) says this: Station 6 takes nothing by hand and the forge offers no rails for it until trains run. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-09-station-6-takes-nothing-by-hand](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-09-station-6-takes-nothing-by-hand).
- **CL-077 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm; the card's words are yours (draft "Steam" / "Trains run both lines."). Every other first find gets a card, and "Rewards should be maximal" (2 November); the moment the trains wake is the biggest one on Home. Source: [docs/plans/boiler-first-choice.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-09](plans/boiler-first-choice.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-09) (call 27) · [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-02-rewards-should-be-maximal](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-02-rewards-should-be-maximal).
- **CL-082 — changed.** Recommendation: change: make forks trades with lasting differences, like the Quick and Strong boilers. You have set order-only choices aside for the garage: on 15 November the per-line trade replaced "which line first", and the focus is real choice. Your 16 November first-island call excuses an order choice only as a first-island teaching beat. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-trade-answers](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-trade-answers) · [docs/decisions-process.md#d-2026-11-16-focus-choice-and-payoff](decisions-process.md#d-2026-11-16-focus-choice-and-payoff) · [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-island-teaches](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-island-teaches).
- **CL-084 — changed.** Recommendation: change: confirm the look and no undo; drop "free" and "first press wins a fork". Installs now spend steel from the one pool (15 November, answer 2), and Quick/Strong are freely swappable trades, not a race to press (15 November trade answers). Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices) · [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-trade-answers](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-trade-answers).
- **CL-091 — changed.** Recommendation: change: confirm the card and prompt words; the hint shows Jump. You ruled "Jump is the glider's button" on 15 November: the first-fall teach shows Jump, not Glide/Q/LB. Source: [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#jump-is-the-gliders-button](agents/owner-calls.md#jump-is-the-gliders-button).

- **CL-107 — reverted.** Recommendation: revert: keep the double jump after Station 3. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-09-loop-orders](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-09-loop-orders) · [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-line-choice-two-stages](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-first-line-choice-two-stages) · [docs/direction.md#owner-decisions-that-change-the-next-work](direction.md#owner-decisions-that-change-the-next-work).

The Home half is already yours (fly first, 15 November); the open half makes the glider a required gate at Ring Island, which sets what the ability is for. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-fly-first](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-fly-first).
- **CL-121 — confirmed.** [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#jump-is-the-gliders-button](agents/owner-calls.md#jump-is-the-gliders-button) — Owner: "Jump is the glider’s button". The entry explicitly states that the first-fall teaching displays Jump, confirming this original glyph choice after an intervening Glide-glyph default.

### Superseded entries
- <a id="d-2026-11-17-heavy-load-pile-rule-as-shipped"></a>**2026-11-17, heavy-load tuning with bounded local drop recovery** (owner, 17 November 2026, review page: "the others I accept all"). Confirm comfort 8/16, maximum 12/24, the 45% linear speed floor and the rack moves as logged. Replace only "Remove the 128-pile ceiling" with the shipped rule: `LOOSE_PILE_CAPACITY = 128`, eight pieces per pile; heavy excess reserves drop slots, pickups are bounded by spare slots, and a whole-pile take may reclaim its source slot. Drops top up same-position piles and use unreserved slots or release their own excess. See [final integration](archive/plans/heavy-load-final.md), `src/sim/railway-supply.ts`, `railway-queries.ts` and `railway-sys-line1.ts`. The ceiling was not removed; no code change is authorized here.
  _Archived 17 November 2026; superseded by Core loop, [2026-11-17, separate wood and stone capacities](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-17-separate-capacities) (owner: "i dont like the encumbrance system as it is")._
- **The mainland port city** is visible from the start, reachable at the end, and you can walk around it.
  _Archived 12 November 2026; superseded by Core loop, 2026-11-12, the campaign outline: seven owner decisions answered (owner, 12 November UTC: the mainland is a backdrop only); the 7 November call in [map-prototypes](world/map-prototypes.md) ("never reached in the main game") stands._
- **Eras:** six: wood, steam, iron and harbour, diesel and concrete, electric (bullet trains with pantographs and overhead wires), and a future train that "doesn't have to be actually an exact monorail".
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Core loop, 2026-11-10, the four era entries (owner: "Archive "Eras: six" and "What ends an era""); eras became better materials (Changed, 2026-11-02, "Materials replace eras")._
- **What ends an era:** reaching a key connection.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Core loop, 2026-11-10, the four era entries (owner: "Archive "Eras: six" and "What ends an era"")._

- **While nobody plays:** the network banks one project, and the next step waits for players. _(Superseded 2026-11-06: an empty world is paused, and the next join applies one capped away credit.)_
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by its own note, "Superseded 2026-11-06: an empty world is paused"; Rewards, 2026-11-06, economy principles amended._
- **The island grows through eight technology eras.** It expands outward (reclaimed land) and new islands rise around it.
  1. **The wood age** (in the game; "castaway camp" is a name for its look only)
  2. Steam wagons
  3. Iron and coal
  4. Steel and harbour
  5. Diesel and concrete
  6. Electric
  7. Monorail city
  8. Maglev
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Core loop, 2026-11-02, the game runs closer to 8 hours ("The eight eras below are the long list, no longer the plan")._

<a id="b2-rest-rail-flag-evidence"></a>

#### Flag dispositions and earlier owner words

- **RC-026 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 15 November garage answer (1): "a Roundhouse bay holds one thing the room owns and is not running and is never bought; each fitted line keeps its own bay". Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices). Earlier owner words, 15 November: "Only doubt is for question 5"; the cited entry records the other four garage recommendations as accepted.
- **RC-031 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: Home's seven bays stand; Port Quay has five bays. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 15 November answer (4): "Port Quay stands complete at arrival, five bays on the decided order". Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices).
- **RC-033 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: its arc is five bays, standing and complete at arrival; the other differences stand. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 15 November answer (4) fixes five bays at Port Quay, not a half ring. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-garage-choices).
- **RC-035 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: keep the clear lane; drop "cradle and table stay NO CUE". Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-027, logged later the same day after your "There is never any prompt to bring 8 rails to the cradle stall", gives both beats a cue. Source: [RC-027](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail), [docs/archive/plans/footings-signalling.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-14](archive/plans/footings-signalling.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-14).
- **RC-047 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 14 November Round blockout answer ("I'll take your recommendations on the other decisions") accepted the rooftop run to the balcony gift until D4. Source: [docs/decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-14-round-blockout-recommendations-accepted](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-14-round-blockout-recommendations-accepted). Earlier owner words, 14 November: "I'll take your recommendations on the other decisions."
- **RC-061 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm the rest; drop the grey stair. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-001 (built, 15 November) removed the stair, and your 15 November call keeps the West landing to stakes and the board until the Bell Rock build. Source: [RC-001](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-16-batch2-rail-calls-confirmed), [docs/decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-15-west-landing-staked-until-bell-rock](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-15-west-landing-staked-until-bell-rock).
- **RC-076 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: the short turn is parked). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-068 parked the short turn after the cheaper Ring Island hold cleared the bar; it returns only if a later leg misses. Source: [RC-068](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail), [docs/plans/short-turn.md#design-ruling-on-step-1s-sheet-designer-2026-11-12](plans/short-turn.md#design-ruling-on-step-1s-sheet-designer-2026-11-12).
- **RC-077 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: used and done). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): The bar was applied: the Ring Island hold cleared it, so it was built (RC-067) and the short turn parked (RC-068). Source: [RC-067](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-16-batch2-rail-calls-confirmed), [RC-068](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-078 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: the short turn is parked). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-068 parked the short turn; the measurement it plans will not run unless a later leg misses. Source: [RC-068](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-079 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: the short turn is parked). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-068 parked the short turn. Source: [RC-068](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-080 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: the short turn is parked). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-068 parked the short turn. Source: [RC-068](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-093 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm, and end the exemption once no footings are owed. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 12 November cargo answer (1) pauses economy stops only "while its wagon is owed footings", which answers the question. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-cargo-trains-and-hub-towns-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-cargo-trains-and-hub-towns-answered).
- **RC-096 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): It bends your 10 November "a request lever at each halt calls the next train to stop", where only the express may run past; it was never built, and the Ring Island hold met the bar instead (RC-067, RC-068). Source: [docs/decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-10-trains-stop-on-request-at-the](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-10-trains-stop-on-request-at-the), [docs/decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-10-open-calls-answered-the-express-and](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-10-open-calls-answered-the-express-and).
- **RC-098 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm the hold as built (RC-067, RC-070): off only for errand, lever-assigned and joining trains themselves. Source: [RC-067](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-16-batch2-rail-calls-confirmed), [RC-070](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-099 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: done). - **RC-101 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (with RC-096). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): It only applies if passing is built; RC-096 recommends against it. Source: [RC-096](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).

- **RC-169 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm, with the blocked line by what the bill lacks. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-157 replaced the single line with "Bring logs", "Bring rails" or "Bring logs or rails". Source: [RC-157](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-189 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: drop the 11 m rule; bought rooftops may reach the balcony; probe the hops as planned. - **RC-192 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: cut the rows; no clock either. Source: [docs/decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-14-the-boogler-s-statue-replaces-the-clock](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-14-the-boogler-s-statue-replaces-the-clock).
- **RC-209 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (superseded). Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered), [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-16-eight-plots-staked-by-deliveries](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-16-eight-plots-staked-by-deliveries).
- **RC-219 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): There is no `?proto=trade`: the town economy was built in the main game (slice 5, #1927 to #1935). Source: [docs/direction.md#now-next-later](direction.md#now-next-later).
- **RC-220 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm at 1 rider a minute. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): You set 1 rider a minute on 16 November (RW-003). - **RC-223 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (superseded). Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-12-campaign-outline-answered](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-12-campaign-outline-answered).

- **RC-328 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm the gap and glide; the brace platform is at 8.0 m on RC-280's bow. Source: [RC-262](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail), [RC-280](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-332 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm, with `WORKS.total` 32. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-268 corrects the total to 32 after your late-game answer removed the cradle's two footings. Source: [RC-268](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).

- **RC-334 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (superseded). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-310 and then RC-280 set the bow to a 4% grade ending at 8.0 m. Source: [RC-310](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail), [RC-280](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-335 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already decided); the names stay the owner's copy. Earlier owner words, 10 November: "well its not ordinary because it's perfect and floatin right?"; "It should be funny and goofy though". Names and character words remain the owner's copy.
- **RC-336 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already decided). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): The 10 November entry's title rule, "steel as the longer boiler", covers it. - **RC-337 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (superseded). - **RC-347 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm, with the lamp on the cab roof's front. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-325 as built puts the stop lamp on the front of the cab roof. Source: [RC-325](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-348 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm, with RC-324's reach. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): RC-324 as built accepts a halt's own reach (about 12 m) and a rider by the train's pose, length and lag. Source: [RC-324](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-356 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: the card is confirmed; it stays off only until you give its words. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-boiler-card-owner-copy](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-16-boiler-card-owner-copy).
- **RC-367 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Layout A is gone: no `?home=` value is left and the old Home is tagged `archive/home-a` (RC-056). Source: [RC-056](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail).
- **RC-375 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: keep the prompt and its reach to the pedestal's edge; a back load is set down beside you instead. Source: [RC-250](decisions-archive.md#b2-rest-rail), [docs/decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-10-open-calls-answered-yards-at-halts](decisions-rail.md#d-2026-11-10-open-calls-answered-yards-at-halts).
- **RC-385 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Earlier owner words, 10 November: "I actually think they should be big enough for the Buddy to fit in the cabin, even though the little guys will drive". The 9 November [three answers](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-09-station-6-takes-nothing-by-hand) record the recommended stall bearings.

- **RC-399 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (superseded). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): The shelf's place is set by the 9 and 10 November entries, and layout A is gone. #### Original status receipts, batch-2 flags and bulk: Rail, construction, levels and travel

These original-status lines preserve the immutable split manifest's exact history. They are fenced receipts, not additional moved calls.

```text
| 2026-11-10 | New: the express's rules as the brief has them: one a line at a time; a 20 s wait that never holds another train; anyone aboard can Go now; a cord pull on the way ends it there; an empty express still runs; a train owing a cargo stop is never claimed; only junction stations call; free with the hall board; no notice to far-away friends; the caller can cancel during the wait and the cord is guarded just after leaving (both added) | [express-train](brainstorms/express-train.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10), calls 15–22 and 24, finding 5 | designer | Bounded and legible; the two additions cover a solo player missing the train and a late Go now becoming a stop request | confirmed (owner 10 November, for the wait only: "20 s with a bell; anyone aboard can go now"; the other rules in this row are still open) |
| 2026-11-10 | New: the hall's live line map gains a pick on the map itself (no list), replacing 9 November's "No fast travel or picker"; the express replaces the 1 November jump and is how an invited friend reaches the others | [express-train](brainstorms/express-train.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10), calls 23 and 32, rulings 5 and 6, question 5 | designer | Owner, 10 November: "Not instant fast travel"; a 2D page comes before the board is built | confirmed (owner 10 November: "Yes: hubs and the board stay, the jump becomes a ride"; for the ride replacing the jump; the pick on the map itself was not asked) |
| 2026-11-10 | Default: the express's dressing and numbers: the 20 s wait with the dwell inside it, three bell rings, the headboard, the last 5 s ticking, whistles, the gold ring on its marker, the board about 10 m from the stop mark on the hall's back span, pad ring and touch tap, dim stops that say why, the cord's glow, no cool-down, a Large install of about 14 s, the Roundhouse listing interchanges, the draft strings | [express-train](brainstorms/express-train.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10), calls 34–43 and 45 | designer | Tunables, placements and copy, cheap to change after play; none of the lines are The Boogler's | open |
| 2026-11-10 | Default: each designed junction station's plan keeps a 40 m bay road with a 65 m parallel throat and 65 m parallel approaches, whether or not a dedicated express set is built | [express-train](brainstorms/express-train.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10), call 44, finding 6 | designer | Free on paper now; the Gorge's 90 m split will not take it later | open |
| 2026-11-10 | New: stations come in three tiers and a halt never gets a station building; a designed station is earned by what the place is (a change of train, a choice or a town), not by a count of lines. Six designed stations, not seven: Home, Ring Island, High Gorge, Bell Rock, Canopy Town, Port Quay. West landing is Home's second front at the Station tier (a kit boathouse house) | [junction-stations](brainstorms/junction-stations.md#design-review-designer-2026-11-10), calls 11–13, ruling 1, question 3 | designer (junction-stations review) | The walk-in rule makes every building an interior, so tiering keeps halts cheap. ### Batch-2 decide calls answered 16 November 2026
```

Owner, in chat: "If I didn't say something else, I took the recommendation". RC-005 and RC-062 have separate page answers; RC-308 has a page answer and the later "4% is ok then" confirmation. Sources: [RC ranking](reports/agent-calls-ranking-rc-2026-11-16.json), [small-topics ranking](reports/agent-calls-ranking-small-2026-11-16.json), [application report](reports/agent-call-batch2-decide-answers-applied-2026-11-16.md). Only the decide group is answered; flags and bulk remain open.

Rows in baseline ID order: RC-001, RC-005, RC-022, RC-062, RC-067, RC-186, RC-221, RC-233, RC-308, RC-313, RC-314. Only the status cell changes, with decision links.

### Superseded entries

- **2026-11-10, idea under brainstorm: call an express at the larger stations, and interchanges between lines** (owner: "It would be cool if from the larger stations, you could call a train and board it to go to any station accessible on the network. Not instant fast travel, but once you select, you board the passenger only train from the rotunda and maybe there's a short wait (so friends can hop on) and then I departs for your destination? what do you think of the feature"; then, told that lines don't physically connect today: "Yeah I feel maybe an interchange is an upgrade that allows you to go from any line to another by having crossovers at junctions?"). An idea being brainstormed, **not a decision to build**. The specifics (the next service train run as the express rather than a separate train, called on a junction station's hall map, a 20 s wait for friends, one express per line, interchanges bought per junction for the express only, Home and Bell Rock never getting one, the express on line 2 from Home first) are the [express-train](brainstorms/express-train.md) brief's proposals, not his picks; its five questions are open. "Not instant fast travel" bears on the 1 November "only hub stations are fast travel" (a jump): the brief reads the express as that call built as a ride. Not built.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Rail, 2026-11-10, open calls answered: the express and interchanges ("This turns the idea logged above ... into a direction to build")._
- **Halt boards show the island map** (9 November, owner: "Island map", picked from four 2D boards in the [halt board prototype](https://claude.ai/artifact/YD9Shqc9px5MV78MRp68kZ), after "The line strip board looks less nice than the old prototype one, where you could see the other stations like a map"). Every halt's board draws both lines where they really run, every station named, this halt ringed, both trains live; unlaid track dashed and its station dim. Supersedes the bare line strip of the same day. The Home hall's big board (plan step 12) draws the same map larger, with no ring ([place](game-design/place.md), "Departure boards at the halts").
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by the longer copy of the same entry (same words, plus the 10 November low display), which stays in Rail._
- **2026-11-01, only hub stations are fast travel** (owner, after the board playtest): fast travel is a jump between locations; other stops are reached by riding. Hubs get the big departures board and more signage; smaller stops get a minimal sign. The train could be faster too. Was "stations are the fast-travel board", see Changed.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Rail, 2026-11-10, open calls answered: the express and interchanges ("Supersedes: 1 November, only hub stations are fast travel")._

- **RW-032 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). You picked the stone stop once the pier's footings are done (12 November) and then defined it as the full-load stop; only the dwell and platform are left, and they are tunables. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered) · [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-cargo-trains-and-hub-towns-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-cargo-trains-and-hub-towns-answered).
- **RW-035 — changed.** Recommendation: change: confirm with 1 rider a minute (as built), the rest as is. RW-003 lowered the rate to 1 a minute, it is built (ridersPerMin 1), and you confirmed it on 16 November. Source: RW-003 · src/sim/railway-trade-layout.ts · [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-16-town-questions-as-recommended](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-16-town-questions-as-recommended).
- **RW-040 — changed.** Recommendation: change: keep eight plots in a fixed order; plots are staked by deliveries, not by arrival. On 12 November you decided deliveries stake the Village's plots; RW-005 also changes which plot comes first. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered).
- **RW-041 — confirmed.** [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-11-boogles-the-five-open-questions-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-11-boogles-the-five-open-questions-answered) — Owner: "I like your recommendations for 1-5". Answer 1 explicitly confirms no personal balances, automatic shared fares and carried coins as props.
- **RW-043 — reverted.** Recommendation: revert (retire; re-run the model). Hand arithmetic from 10 November, before your 11 November doubling curve; you left the late-game shortfall as an open content question. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-11-boogles-prices-are-stand-ins-to](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-11-boogles-prices-are-stand-ins-to).
- **RW-044 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm. Besides houses these are the only things money buys, so they are most of the payoff menu; you confirmed the loader part, and the steps and numbers are open. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-10-boogles-build-houses-and-upgrade-sources](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-10-boogles-build-houses-and-upgrade-sources).
- **RW-045 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm, as model inputs only. It shapes the late game, which you left open as a content question; nothing should be built on it before you pick the goals. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-11-boogles-prices-are-stand-ins-to](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-11-boogles-prices-are-stand-ins-to).
- **RW-048 — confirmed.** Recommendation: confirm. Players will want to know what the room has while far from Home; putting it on signs in the world follows your signs-first call, and Pause may show it. Source: [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#world-signs](agents/owner-calls.md#world-signs).


History for [decisions-rewards.md](decisions-rewards.md), verbatim.

### Superseded entries

- **Three things you earn, none of them a shared wallet** (som and knacks parked, see the audit picks) _(superseded 2026-11-06: progression and the wallet are shared; secret-crate finds and some cosmetics stay personal)_:
  - **shared progress**, the island's;
  - **som**, a personal coin, earned only and spent on looks;
  - **knacks**, personal skill per activity, which unlock options and never power.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by its own note, "superseded 2026-11-06: progression and the wallet are shared"; Rewards, 2026-11-06, economy principles amended._

### Superseded entries
- **2026-11-03, demo sky with AgX is the best starting point for the look** (owner, after the `&sky=demo&tone=agx` boards: "so far I really like Demo Sky AGX"). A frozen late morning (`tod=0.24`) is the comparison's pick. Rolling it out goes with the real-sea work; expect to tune trees and the avatar under it.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Look, 2026-11-10, the sky is the gradient and cloud sprites (owner: "The 10 November gradient and cloud sprites, in the lighter blue, lit by the 6 November cell 5 settings; the demo-sky and bake entries are archived")._
- **2026-11-03, bake the real-water sky once during loading, at late morning** (owner). Under `?water=new`, the demo sky and metered AgX are the production starting look, frozen at `tod=0.24`. Its cube is baked once behind the loading screen and never during play, with the real sun fixed to that direction; it replaces the toon dome, sun/moon billboards and cycle on this route. More baked times may be considered later. The old route retains Neutral and its cycle until the reviewed switch (water-graduation step 7); `scene.ts` stays unchanged.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Look, 2026-11-10, the sky is the gradient and cloud sprites (owner: "The 10 November gradient and cloud sprites, in the lighter blue, lit by the 6 November cell 5 settings; the demo-sky and bake entries are archived")._
- **2026-11-03, the real sea uses the demo's sky, with time of day frozen at a pleasant state** (owner): change the time only intentionally, in parts of the game. It replaces the 20-minute cycle, so the Design Table's day-only and night-only things hang off those chosen moments. Bake each chosen time once, ahead of time.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Look, 2026-11-10, the sky is the gradient and cloud sprites (owner: "The 10 November gradient and cloud sprites, in the lighter blue, lit by the 6 November cell 5 settings; the demo-sky and bake entries are archived"). Its time-of-day rule (change the time only on purpose) lives on in [the room owns the time of day](decisions-look.md#d-2026-11-04-the-room-owns-the-time-of) (4 November)._
- **2026-11-03, use the Ultra Water demo sky, frozen at a pleasant time** (owner: "Let's just go with the demo sky and see what it looks like. Can you give me some comparisons?"). Time changes only on purpose. First compare it with the toy characters under Neutral and AgX; `sky=demo` is the opt-in comparison, not the final integration. This supersedes the earlier decision to keep the original sky below. The reference is `/Users/matiasfranco/Developer/boogle-codex/demo-shots/board.png`.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Look, 2026-11-10, the sky is the gradient and cloud sprites (owner: "The 10 November gradient and cloud sprites, in the lighter blue, lit by the 6 November cell 5 settings; the demo-sky and bake entries are archived")._

- **2026-11-10, toon shading style B is the default look, without its rim** _(superseded the same day: baseline shading again, toon B removed)_ (owner, answering the [toon-shading](research/toon-shading.md) boards: "Let's go with shading style B and clean up"). Every Standard-lit surface, characters and environment alike, gets B's soft step on the sun's diffuse, the flattened sky fill and the cool shade side (a patch on the Standard lighting model, now removed; no draws, no pass). The rim is off: this is the orchestrator's reading, not the owner's words. The recommendation he was answering was B with `toonRim=0`, and the standing 2 November call says Buddy has no rim light, so `toon.rim` stays a tunable defaulting to 0, one line to turn on if he asks. Candidates A (characters only) and C (B plus the ink hull) and the `?toon=a|b|c` flag are removed; `?toon=off` keeps three's plain lighting for before/after looks. This supersedes, for the lighting model only, the 4 November "Buddy keeps his current lighting" call: B's step is softer than the two-band ramp turned down then and keeps 30% of the cosine on the lit side.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by its own note, "superseded the same day"; Look, 2026-11-10, baseline shading is the default again; toon B removed._
- **2026-11-03, the water uses the demo's mobile settings on mobile and its default on desktop, as a starting point** (owner). Cost work keeps the look and aims to leave margin for the rest of the game's graphics, which get more advanced over time.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Look, 2026-11-10, the sea is rebuilt small (Ultra Water deleted; "Supersedes ... the 4 and 3 November calls' Ultra water")._
- **2026-11-03, the original sky and clouds stay; `?proto=sky` is parked** (owner, after that pass's final comparison: "it still feels like the original is my preference. I like the original cloud shape more, I think."; "let's park this for now"). The game keeps today's sky, faceted clouds and horizon. The rounded clouds and soft horizon stay behind the flag and don't go into the game.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Look, 2026-11-03, use the Ultra Water demo sky ("This supersedes the earlier decision to keep the original sky below")._

<a id="b2-rest-controls-flag-evidence"></a>

#### Flag dispositions and earlier owner words

- **HU-001 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): You answered on 15 November: "the Far Head cutscene could be longer, that's fine". It is built (#1870). Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-grant-queue-answered](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-grant-queue-answered), [docs/plans/forge-call-handoff.md#as-built-15-november-2026](plans/forge-call-handoff.md#as-built-15-november-2026). Earlier owner words, 15 November: "the Far Head cutscene could be longer, that's fine".
- **HU-002 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: keep the turn toward the forge; the pier's cut shows the breaker waiting, not being cast. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): On 15 November you moved the casting to the boiler fit, "with no camera cut"; the pier cut that is still in code (pier-view.ts) now only shows a breaker already cast, so the row's wording is out of date. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-works-flow](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-works-flow).
- **HU-007 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 11 November acts entry says it ("a returning player gets the act as the first line of the Welcome back card instead; two full-screen moments never stack"), and your later answer that day ("keep it a bit mysterious") already covers the Welcome back line. Source: [docs/decisions-controls.md#d-2026-11-11-the-game-has-acts](decisions-controls.md#d-2026-11-11-the-game-has-acts). Earlier owner words, 11 November: "Sure, makes sense" on withholding unbuilt act cards, then "keep it a bit mysterious". The cited entry records the act as the first Welcome back line and no stacked full-screen moments.
- **HU-018 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: out of date). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): The summit switch it describes is gone: on 15 November the switch moved to the sea gate and Q4 got its own camera shot of the jamb. Any clipping in the new shot is a fresh bug, not this call. Source: [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-fly-first](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-fly-first), [docs/decisions-controls.md#d-2026-11-15-q4-gets-a-camera-shot](decisions-controls.md#d-2026-11-15-q4-gets-a-camera-shot).

Median over sessions, n of 2 or more to calibrate. The calibrated run scales every walk of a node by its median (grind too, though the factor is measured on all walking, not loaded carries). <a id="b2-rest-process-flag-evidence"></a>

#### Flag dispositions and earlier owner words

- **AT-003 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm, minus "wardrobe finds stay", with question 1 answered. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): On 13 November you dropped the wardrobe rule ("wardrobe fine stay doesn't need to be a rule") and answered question 1: the dev key holder may replace any room, the shared island included, behind the typed name, countdown and backup. Source: [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#one-room](agents/owner-calls.md#one-room), [docs/plans/room-fast-forward.md#owner-questions](plans/room-fast-forward.md#owner-questions).
- **AT-010 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm the integer counts; drop the `?proto=trade` prototype. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): The town economy shipped straight into the live game (#1927–#1935) and no trade prototype exists in src; your 15 November answer prefers testing in the main game. Source: [docs/direction.md#now-next-later](direction.md#now-next-later), [docs/decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-grant-queue-answered](decisions-core-loop.md#d-2026-11-15-grant-queue-answered).
- **AT-015 — changed.** Accepted recommendation: change: confirm reading cues from the code; the gold ghost is now decided. Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): On 16 November you made the dashed gold outline the game's sign for "a part goes here", so the reason is out of date; the tool still reports only what the code shows. Source: [docs/decisions-look.md#d-2026-11-16-gold-outline-not-physical](decisions-look.md#d-2026-11-16-gold-outline-not-physical).
- **AT-031 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: superseded). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): Your 13 November worktree rule removes unmerged worktrees "without waiting 48 hours", tagging uncommitted work first; this was a one-off from 9 November. Source: [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#worktrees](agents/owner-calls.md#worktrees).
- **AT-034 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (retire: done). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): The town rows exist (Timber home, Fares paid, Ready to build, House built in src/world/railway-jump.ts); under "One live game", later stages go in as fast-forward stages, not new jump-in rooms. Source: src/world/railway-jump.ts, [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#one-room](agents/owner-calls.md#one-room).
- **AT-050 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): You answered on 12 November that "deliveries stake the plots", and the slice 5 freight is built. Source: [docs/decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered](decisions-rewards.md#d-2026-11-12-town-economy-answered). Earlier owner choice, 12 November: the recommended "deliveries stake the plots" option. His cargo words were "I feel cago trains should be stopping at all resource sources to pick up cargo. player shouldnt have to be carrying that".
- **AT-070 — reverted.** Accepted recommendation: revert (superseded). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): On 14 November you said it "feels a little cramped" and "the train barely fits", and asked for a real rotunda, bigger, reusable across the hubs. Source: [docs/agents/owner-calls.md#the-roundhouse-is-a-real-rotunda](agents/owner-calls.md#the-roundhouse-is-a-real-rotunda).
- **AT-073 — confirmed.** Accepted recommendation: confirm and move to the archive (already answered). Ranking rationale (the designer's wording): You decided it on 8 November: "backdrop for now is fine"; areas became walkable as they were built, as that entry says. Source: [docs/decisions-process.md#d-2026-11-08-the-sickle-isles-map-as-a](decisions-process.md#d-2026-11-08-the-sickle-isles-map-as-a). Earlier owner words, 8 November: "backdrop for now is fine"; "the full map will have to run on mobile anyways too".

#### Original status receipts, batch-2 flags and bulk: Architecture, tools and prototype lifecycle

These original-status lines preserve the immutable split manifest's exact history. They are fenced receipts, not additional moved calls.

```text
| 2026-11-09 | `loop -- trace` downloads a missing session with `npm run playtest`'s own path, now `dev/playtest/fetch.ts` (`playdata()`, `resolveSession`), into this checkout's `.look/playtest`; `--since` is a UTC date over the server's newest 200 sessions, people only (`meta.bot` off), and offline it reads local files from that time. The aggregate counts a node for a session when that session has a window, marks or carries at it; confusion is summed and ranked, wayfinding is the median walked/pred over canonical-segment windows | `dev/loop/trace.ts`, `dev/playtest/fetch.ts` | implementer | One download path; the list endpoint already caps at 200 | open |
| 2026-11-09 | Loop detour factors: a node's sample is its window's walked metres (1 Hz samples, jumps of 40 m or more skipped, so a train ride still counts as walking) over the prediction's straight-line metres per player at that session's player count, for the same canonical segment only: the window must open on the node reached before it, that node must come earlier in the canonical order, the window must last 5 s or more, and the segment adds only optional nodes the session never reached; a segment the prediction walks under 20 m is skipped. Median over sessions, n of 2 or more to calibrate. The calibrated run scales every walk of a node by its median (grind too, though the factor is measured on all walking, not loaded carries). Left out: `meta.bot`, rooms starting `proto`, older mark names (`stretch laid` + id, `ability gained`, `train unlocked`), and sessions that reach no node | `dev/loop/calibrate.ts`, `dev/loop/trace.ts` | implementer | Comparing a window with a different stretch of the prediction gave factors of x0.00 and x0.33 that only measured the order played | open |
```

### Superseded entries

- **2026-11-08, playtest checkpoint: pause after the hook shot and Q5** (owner: "create a checkpoint where it makes sense to fully pause development until I playtest"). Once the hook shot and line 2 part 2's B4 (Q5, the sea gate, Far Head) are on main and verified, development stops fully: no new tasks, no merges, no world resets, until the owner has played. The checkpoint covers one arc, from line 1 and the forge through Q1–Q4 with the cart and pack, the hook-shot climb up Needle Bell and Q5's trestle to Far Head, plus the unplayed great beam, co-op abilities, seats, chairs and longer view. Before stopping, the orchestrator publishes a playtest guide with what to try and what to look for. This replaces part 2's "one playtest at the pier" stop for now; B5–B8 resume after the owner's verdict.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Process, 2026-11-09, nothing waits for a playtest ("Supersedes ... the 8 November playtest checkpoint")._
- **2026-11-02, be cautious removing prototypes for now** (owner: "let's be cautious about removing the prototypes for now"; `place` is in active use). No further archiving without asking, whatever the rule below would allow.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Process, 2026-11-06, conform to tested engine architecture, "No prototypes on `main`" ("Supersedes 2 November's "be cautious removing prototypes" and its archive policy")._
- **2026-11-02, finished prototypes are archived, not kept on `main`** (owner: "I just don't know if I want prototype behavior in mainline"; "I don't care about removing separate branches"; "agreed archive"). Being tested, or depended on: on `main` behind its flag. Answered with nothing depending on it: saved as a tag `archive/<name>` and removed. First: `archive/board` (with the board whoosh the owner kept, which nothing else plays; the hubs' big board gets built for real), `archive/moves` (double jump and glide, for when they come back as unlocks) and `archive/groove` (the music clock, grid and stems loader, for the owner's own music). `?proto=lay` stays as the tester for the real laying run. `wagon`, `era`, `haul`, `minis` and `crossing` wait, because `net` and `place` use them.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Process, 2026-11-06, conform to tested engine architecture, "No prototypes on `main`" (the archive policy it supersedes)._
- **2026-11-01, playtests are solo-first.** Each find-the-fun question must be answerable by one person; multiplayer comes after. (Replaces "playtest with 2–4 people", see Changed.)
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Process, 2026-11-07, evaluate shared progression adventures ("superseding "solo first"")._
- **2026-11-01, the RC-car soapbox stays** ("pretty fun"; was "one more try, then cut"). Races with these carts are an idea for later (ideas.md). Not being able to drive on at the bottom of the hill felt like a possible miss, so the next version lets you keep driving.
  _Archived 10 November 2026; superseded by Process, 2026-11-02, the soapbox is removed ("Replaces the entry below")._

## Changed

- **2026-11-09:** The 5 November whistle pick ("Call cart to forge" with a bell and delivery cues) and cart-whistle variant A's handle became "cut the handle"; the delivery cues (the rack status) stay (Trains and the world, 9 November).
- **2026-11-09:** "Stop at the owner's playtest" (3 November, owner-calls) and the holds built on it ("nothing merges while the owner is playing", 5 November; "playtest checkpoint: pause after the hook shot and Q5" and Split-rock's rules merging only after the owner has played B5–B8, 8 November) became "nothing waits for a playtest" (Process, 9 November). Only a `WORLD_NS` bump waits while the owner is mid-session.
- **2026-11-07:** Replaced "solo first" with evaluating solo and co-op alongside each other. Accepted whole-project progression, economy and brief-review recommendations; approved delivery ownership and sequencing continue (Process).
- **2026-11-07:** Retired "Toys before chores" and "Cooperation is automatic, not negotiated" as the vision changed. Their former constraints and historical citations no longer bind design; no replacement principles were adopted (Process).
- **2026-11-07:** Drop-in co-op became a secondary consideration as the game's vision evolved. Principle 8 is now "Enjoyable alone and together"; late-arrival onboarding is no longer a milestone requirement. The owner accepted the work → machinery → exploration proof recommendations with this amendment (Process).
- **2026-11-07:** The owner clarified that "Claude subagents for work, Codex for images" was a soft suggestion. Codex is available for general work; choose by task fit and respect existing orchestration ownership (owner-calls).
- **2026-11-06:** "Codex first" became "Claude subagents for work, Codex for images" (owner-calls), "be cautious removing prototypes" became "no prototypes on `main`", and "still no ECS" was reopened for evaluation (Process, 6 November).
- **2026-11-02:** "no hazards at all, nothing decays" was withdrawn along with DESIGN.md's "Nothing is the antagonist": the owner called the guidance outdated. Danger is open; nothing replaces it yet.
- **2026-11-02:** "the island grows through technology eras" became "you find better materials and upgrade": the world doesn't change era, your things do. The era lists above are history; asset limits and the round structure still apply.
- **2026-11-02:** "curios to collect" became "abilities to find": what you discover opens new paths.
- **2026-11-02:** "the ending is parked" became "it ends on the original island, meeting The Boogler". All other narrative detail stays parked.
- **2026-11-02:** "the hotbar stays for now" became "one thing in your hands": the owner played `?proto=onehand` and didn't miss it.
- **2026-11-02:** "eight eras at 1–3 evenings each" became "closer to 8 hours, starting with fewer eras": the owner wants to limit how many assets the game needs.
- **2026-11-01:** "riding must be more than standing on a moving platform" became "the wagon is transportation": after trying the research's variants, the owner doesn't want extra verbs on the ride.
- **2026-11-01:** "stations are the fast-travel board" became "only hub stations are fast travel", after the board playtest: every stop being a jump would make riding pointless.
- **2026-11-01:** the synthesized groove loop was cut after the listening pack.
- **2026-11-01:** "Playtest constantly with 2–4 real people" (DESIGN.md, How we work) became "solo first": the owner wants each prototype to work for one person before building out.
- **2026-11-01:** the haul stack's sway and jump spill (0.1 groundwork) were cut after the first solo haul playtest: the owner wants a plain back bundle.
- **2026-11-01:** the asset pipeline moved from "when the first authored model lands" to now, so Blender work can start as soon as a look is picked.
- **2026-11-01:** implementers moved from Sonnet to Opus at medium effort.

## Current-decision index

_Retired 10 November 2026, when the log was split by topic._

Routing updated 7 November 2026. Read only the linked sections and dated entries relevant to the task. Later explicit owner calls win; this index grants no new scope or proof of implementation. [Owner calls](agents/owner-calls.md) bind every task; [DESIGN](../DESIGN.md) owns principles, plans own tasks, and [game-design](game-design.md) records what exists. The dated record below remains history.

### Current build and next round

[The shape of the game](#the-shape-of-the-game): **3–4 November, default switch / Place gets the top-level room** sets the bare `boogle` entry and fresh progress. **4 November, production core scope and follow-up** starts headless slice 1, moves across main and drops the T1-only stop. [Process](#process): **6 November, start the line-2 move now** and **7 November, keep going while the owner is away** govern work: ECS E, Sickle Isles greybox, then its economy prototype. **7 November, next proof / evaluate shared progression adventures** refines the review. Owner-calls: since **9 November, nothing waits for a playtest**. **10 November, open calls answered** tops Decided.

### Core loop, pacing and progression

[The shape of the game](#the-shape-of-the-game): **2 November, direction after round 3 / Design Table**, plus **3 November, after round 4 / trees**. Building, exploration and incremental growth matter equally; path first, train later; staged efficiency. [Trains and the world](#trains-and-the-world): **6 November, ring-and-economy answers** sets roughly six hours, mostly linear progression with returns and eventual automation. [Process](#process): **7 November, retire principles 1 and 4 / evaluate shared progression adventures** permits reward-dependent mechanics and deliberate coordination; judge whole projects and solo/co-op alongside each other. Earlier toy-first and solo-first tests no longer bind. [Changed](#changed) records superseded eras and curios.

### Rail, construction, levels and travel

[Trains and the world](#trains-and-the-world): **2 November** physical construction, solid objects, visible cargo/town growth, crossing and sitting decisions; sitting does not restore the cut bench camera. **6 November** sets Split-rock, Ring Island and the smallest Roundhouse after line-2 playtest. **7 November, great beam** allows any axe and delivery order, rope drag, and the proper axe at the forge. [Process](#process) routes the Sickle Isles greybox. **3 November, after round 4** removes the objective marker ([place](game-design/place.md#the-run)); older signs/beacons are history. Free track remains direction, not a level specification; maps/fast-travel research stays deferred by the Design Table call.
### Rewards, co-op and town roles
